NOTE (disk): several files in this directory stall on read (iCloud Desktop sync); hashes were taken via direct `shasum` with extended timeouts and cross-checked two ways for the large files; both methods agree except where noted. `tx_dtpa_ch17_statutes.html` / `tx_tdpsa_ch541_statutes.htm` (250,874 B, identical MD5 efea928e141b80aab41ab6d59381c80a): both are Angular JS shells of statutes.capitol.texas.gov (no statute text) — superseded by the TCSS resources .htm captures in rows 471/472; retained as access inventory, do not cite. `47usc227_cornell.html` (249,144 B): § 227 robocalls — captured by the FCC-enforcement leg for its auto-dialer/recordkeeping context (do not cite for accessibility). `adagov_sec_compliance_guide.html` (107,352 B): ADA.gov Small Entity Compliance Guide for the **Title II** web/mobile rule (state/local govts), not the 2022 Title II/III web guidance; superseded for the web-guidance slot by row 474 (which is the DOJ "Guidance on Web Accessibility and the ADA" page). `ftc_deception_policy_statement.html` (1,301 B) is a FTC 404 error body — do not cite (FTC deception policy sourced elsewhere in dossier). `47cfr_part14_ecfr.html` (192,814 B) eCFR Part 14 landing snapshot: content-bearing but pre-render shell + content mix; use rows 470/478 for clean full text.
